# Track B local work catalogue

**Prepared:** 23 September 2026  
**Source folder:** `/Users/cobro/Documents/Work`  
**Purpose:** locate local examples for Track B’s spreadsheet and PowerPoint output-quality study, with their adjacent analysis files and visual assets.

## Coverage

I recursively walked the complete source tree: **29,493 files in 2,922 directories** (including the source root), spanning 95 file extensions. The complete inventory below covers **3,684 files in 326 directories**: spreadsheets and presentations; SAV, Q, and Tableau/Power BI analysis files; CSV, PDF, Word, text, and archive files; and image/chart-template assets. The [folder census](folder_census.json) records direct file and extension counts for every directory, including those with no catalogue-format files.

This is an exhaustive **folder and filename/format inventory** for the listed formats, not a claim that I visually reviewed every item. Only a subset has been content-checked and rendered; the notes below name exactly which files were previewed. All remaining files stay indexed for follow-up and do not yet have quality labels. Other file types—such as code, email, and application data—are included in the census but not treated as output examples. `BadgeSwift` alone accounts for 25,622 files. Its 14 indexed `.txt` files are third-party dependency notices; it contains no spreadsheet, presentation, survey-data, Q, Tableau, PDF, CSV, or Word report examples.

No source files or respondent/customer-level rows were copied. File links work only on this machine. The collection includes internal company files and third-party material; reuse/distribution rights have **not** been checked. Dates in filenames are clues, not verified publication dates.
